# Skill Select — Claude Code & ECC Skills Vault Dispatcher & Router

`skill_select` is the central intelligence and routing hub for the **Claude Code & ECC Skills Vault** (760+ skills). Its job is to eliminate decision paralysis, solve context window limitations (which truncate available skills in default system prompts), and accurately route any engineering, research, security, design, or project task to the best possible skill or multi-skill pipeline.

---

## The 4-Step Skill Selection Framework

When invoked or faced with any complex, ambiguous, or multi-faceted task, follow this systematic 4-step selection process:

### Step 1: Identify Task Archetype & Domain
Determine the primary nature of the task:
- **ECC Engineering System & Quality**: TDD, verification loops, quality gates, code tours -> `tdd-workflow`, `verification-loop`, `gateguard`, `delivery-gate`, `test-collaboration`
- **Framework & Language Patterns**: Idiomatic patterns and TDD for Django, Laravel, Spring Boot, Quarkus, Rails, React, Vue, Angular, Kotlin, Swift, Rust, Go, C++, Perl -> `django-tdd`, `laravel-tdd`, `springboot-tdd`, `react-patterns`, `golang-patterns`, `rust-testing`, `swiftui-patterns`
- **Security & Hardening**: Vulnerability discovery, AgentShield, OWASP scanning, diff scanning, threat modeling, DeFi/LLM security -> `deep-security-scan`, `security-review`, `threat-model`, `defi-amm-security`, `llm-trading-agent-security`
- **Agent Harness & Metacognition**: Stack audit, self-debugging, harness construction, eval harness, shared memory -> `agent-architecture-audit`, `agent-introspection-debugging`, `agent-harness-construction`, `agent-eval`, `unified-memory`
- **Project Orchestration**: Multi-phase milestones, planning, state tracking -> `gsd-*` suite
- **Parallel Multi-Agent Coding (Swarm)**: One task split across parallel workers in isolated git worktrees, shared board, adversarial verification -> `swarm` (bigger scripted fan-outs: `dynamic-workflow`; sequential plan execution: `subagent-driven-development`)
- **Minimalist Code Modification**: Fast, surgically scoped, YAGNI solutions -> `ponytail`, `karpathy-guidelines`
- **AI & Model Engineering**: Model serving, fine-tuning, evals, synthetic data -> `vllm`, `axolotl`, `dspy`, `unsloth`
- **Frontend & Visuals**: Modern UI, dark theme, interactive diagrams, mockups -> `popular-web-designs`, `frontend-ui-engineering`, `excalidraw`
- **Media & Synthesis**: Video, audio, music generation, transcripts -> `whisper`, `manim-video`, `audiocraft`
- **Web Scraping & Intelligence**: Deep research, anti-bot scraping, news monitoring -> `scrapling`, `searxng-search`, `deep-research`, `exa-search`
- **Productivity & Documents**: Excel workbooks, Word docs, PowerPoint, Notion -> `excel-author`, `docx`, `pdf`

### Step 2: Calibrate Operational Constraints & Work Style
Select the approach that matches the user's operational priority:
- **Maximum Speed & Minimal Diff**: Use `ponytail` (lazy senior dev mode, stdlib first) or `gsd-fast` (trivial tasks, inline).
- **Zero-Defect & Continuous Verification**: Use ECC `tdd-workflow` (RED-GREEN) -> `verification-loop` -> `delivery-gate` / `gateguard`.
- **Exploratory & De-risking**: Use `spike` (throwaway experimental spikes) or `sketch` (HTML variants) before building.
- **Deep Architecture & Memory Continuity**: Use `unified-memory` (ECC Memory Vault), `context-carrier`, or `reverse-engineer-anything`.

### Step 3: Compose Multi-Skill Pipelines (Proven Recipes)
Most significant tasks succeed best through a complementary chain of 2–4 skills:

1. **Standard ECC TDD & Quality Feature Pipeline**:
   `contract-first` -> `tdd-workflow` (or framework TDD) -> `verification-loop` -> `code-review` -> `security-diff-scan`
2. **ECC Agent Architecture Hardening Pipeline**:
   `agent-architecture-audit` -> `agent-harness-construction` -> `agent-self-evaluation` -> `eval-harness`
3. **Fast Minimalist Bugfix Pipeline**:
   `systematic-debugging` (isolate root cause) -> `ponytail` (apply simplest fix) -> `karpathy-guidelines`
4. **Full-Spectrum Security Audit & Remediation Pipeline**:
   `deep-security-scan` -> `threat-model` -> `validation` -> `fix-finding` -> `verify-fix` -> `security-and-hardening`
5. **Autonomous Milestone Delivery Pipeline**:
   `gsd-new-milestone` -> `gsd-plan-phase` -> `gsd-execute-phase` -> `gsd-verify-work` -> `gsd-ship`
6. **Modern Web UI & Frontend Pipeline**:
   `popular-web-designs` (select design system) -> `frontend-ui-engineering` -> `browser-testing-with-devtools` -> `accessibility`
7. **Cross-Harness Memory & Context Preservation Pipeline**:
   `unified-memory` -> `context-carrier` -> `context-budget` -> `continuous-learning-v2`
8. **Performance Optimization & Latency Pipeline**:
   `benchmark-optimization-loop` -> `latency-critical-systems` -> `parallel-execution-optimizer` -> `verification-loop`
9. **Parallel Swarm Delivery Pipeline**:
   `planning-and-task-breakdown` (split into disjoint pieces) -> `swarm` (workers in worktrees + board + verifiers) -> `code-review` -> `security-diff-scan`

### Step 4: Skill Activation Protocol
Once a skill is selected:
1. Open and inspect the skill's `SKILL.md` path.
2. Strictly adopt its guidelines, scripts, checklists, and guardrails.
3. If chaining skills, transition between them cleanly and record outcomes.
